"""FastAPI endpoints for the Telegram Football Manager Mini App."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from functools import wraps
from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import InterfaceError, SQLAlchemyError

from .config import settings
from .db import SessionLocal, init_db
from .services import buy_player, market_players, make_transfer_offer, accept_transfer_offer, train_squad, upgrade_stadium, add_copy_to_club
from .game.packs import list_packs, open_pack
from .game.economy import stadium_capacity

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
STATIC_DIR = BASE_DIR / "mini_app" / "dist"
app = FastAPI(title="Football Manager API", version="2.0.0")
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"], allow_credentials=False,
                   allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "OPTIONS"], allow_headers=["*"])


@app.on_event("startup")
async def initialize_database_schema():
    # The API may start before the bot process; make sure all migrations are applied.
    await init_db()


class TacticsPayload(BaseModel):
    formation: str = Field(default="4-3-3", min_length=3, max_length=10)
    style: str = Field(default="BALANCE", min_length=3, max_length=20)
    tempo: int = Field(default=50, ge=0, le=100)
    pressing: int = Field(default=50, ge=0, le=100)
    width: int = Field(default=50, ge=0, le=100)
    defensive_line: int = Field(default=50, ge=0, le=100)
    aggression: int = Field(default=50, ge=0, le=100)


class BidPayload(BaseModel):
    player_id: int = Field(gt=0)


class CreateClubPayload(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=20)
    emblem_shape: str
    emblem_color: str
    emblem_symbol: str


class EmblemPayload(BaseModel):
    emblem_shape: str
    emblem_color: str
    emblem_symbol: str


class ManagerProfilePayload(BaseModel):
    manager_name: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=32)


class LineupPayload(BaseModel):
    player_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=11, max_length=11)
    formation: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=3, max_length=10)


EMBLEM_SHAPES = {"shield", "circle", "diamond", "triangle", "star", "square"}
EMBLEM_COLORS = {"red", "blue", "green", "yellow", "purple", "orange", "black", "white"}
EMBLEM_SYMBOLS = {"ball", "lion", "eagle", "wolf", "lightning", "crown", "star"}


def retry_on_interface_error(endpoint):
    """Retry an HTTP endpoint once if its DB connection was closed unexpectedly."""
    @wraps(endpoint)
    async def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
        try:
            return await endpoint(*args, **kwargs)
        except InterfaceError:
            log.warning("DB connection closed, retrying...")
            return await endpoint(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapped


async def authenticated_telegram_user(x_telegram_init_data: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate signed Telegram initData; never accept unsigned user IDs."""
    init_data = x_telegram_init_data.strip() if isinstance(x_telegram_init_data, str) else ""
    try:
        return validate_telegram_init_data(init_data, settings.bot_token)
    except TelegramInitDataError as exc:
        # Log only a reason code. Never log the raw initData, hash, token, or user payload.
        reason = str(exc)
        log.warning("Telegram Mini App authentication rejected (reason=%s)", reason)
        if reason == "missing":
            detail = "Откройте приложение из Telegram: initData отсутствует."
        elif reason == "bot_token_missing":
            detail = "На сервере не настроен BOT_TOKEN."
        elif reason == "signature_mismatch":
            detail = "Invalid Telegram initData signature. Проверьте, что BOT_TOKEN в Amvera совпадает с токеном этого бота."
        elif reason == "expired":
            detail = "Telegram initData устарел. Полностью закройте и снова откройте Mini App из Telegram."
        else:
            detail = "Invalid Telegram initData (reason: %s). Откройте Mini App заново из Telegram." % reason
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=detail) from exc


async def user_and_club(user: dict[str, Any], session):
    row = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT u.id AS user_id, c.* FROM users u
        LEFT JOIN clubs c ON c.owner_user_id=u.id
        WHERE u.telegram_id=:telegram_id ORDER BY c.is_test ASC, c.id LIMIT 1"""),
        {"telegram_id": user["telegram_id"]})).mappings().first()
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User or club not found; create a club first")
    if row.get("id") is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Club not found; create a club first")
    return row


@app.get("/api/health")
async def health():
    """Liveness probe: the API process is responsive."""
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.get("/api/ready")
async def readiness():
    """Readiness probe: report ready only when PostgreSQL is reachable."""
    try:
        async with SessionLocal() as session:
            await session.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        log.exception("Readiness check failed: PostgreSQL unavailable")
        return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"status": "not_ready", "database": "unavailable"})
    return {"status": "ready", "database": "ok"}


@app.get("/api/me")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def get_me(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        row = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT u.id AS user_id, COALESCE(u.manager_name,u.first_name) AS manager_name,
                c.id AS club_id, c.name AS club_name
            FROM users u LEFT JOIN clubs c ON c.owner_user_id=u.id
            WHERE u.telegram_id=:telegram_id ORDER BY c.is_test ASC, c.id LIMIT 1"""),
            {"telegram_id": user["telegram_id"]})).mappings().first()
        if not row:
            user_id = (await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO users(telegram_id,username,first_name)
                VALUES(:t,:u,:f) ON CONFLICT(telegram_id) DO UPDATE SET username=EXCLUDED.username,
                first_name=EXCLUDED.first_name RETURNING id"""),
                {"t": user["telegram_id"], "u": user.get("username"), "f": user.get("first_name") or "Manager"})).scalar_one()
            await session.commit()
            return {"telegram_id": user["telegram_id"], "user_id": int(user_id), "club_id": None, "club_name": None,
                    "first_name": user.get("first_name") or "Менеджер", "username": user.get("username")}
        return {"telegram_id": user["telegram_id"], "user_id": int(row["user_id"]),
                "club_id": int(row["club_id"]) if row["club_id"] is not None else None,
                "club_name": row["club_name"], "first_name": row["manager_name"] or user.get("first_name") or "Менеджер",
                "username": user.get("username")}


@app.post("/api/manager/profile")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def update_manager_profile(payload: ManagerProfilePayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    name = " ".join(payload.manager_name.split()).strip()
    if len(name) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Имя менеджера должно содержать минимум 2 символа.")
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        await session.execute(text("UPDATE users SET manager_name=:name WHERE telegram_id=:tg"),
                              {"name": name, "tg": int(user["telegram_id"])})
        await session.commit()
    return {"status": "updated", "manager_name": name}


@app.post("/api/club/emblem")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def update_club_emblem(payload: EmblemPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    if payload.emblem_shape not in EMBLEM_SHAPES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Неизвестная форма эмблемы.")
    if payload.emblem_color not in EMBLEM_COLORS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Неизвестный цвет эмблемы.")
    if payload.emblem_symbol not in EMBLEM_SYMBOLS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Неизвестный символ эмблемы.")
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        await session.execute(text("UPDATE clubs SET emblem_shape=:shape,emblem_color=:color,emblem_symbol=:symbol WHERE id=:club"),
            {"shape": payload.emblem_shape, "color": payload.emblem_color, "symbol": payload.emblem_symbol, "club": int(club["id"])})
        await session.commit()
    return {"status": "updated", "emblem_shape": payload.emblem_shape,
            "emblem_color": payload.emblem_color, "emblem_symbol": payload.emblem_symbol}


@app.get("/api/lineup")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def get_starting_lineup(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        tactics = (await session.execute(text("SELECT formation FROM club_tactics WHERE club_id=:c"), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        formation = str(tactics["formation"] if tactics else "4-3-3")
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,p.portrait_url,
                ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)::int AS overall,
                cp.fitness,cp.form,cp.morale,cp.is_injured,COALESCE(cp.suspension_matches,0) AS suspension_matches,
                p.pace,p.shooting,p.passing,p.dribbling,p.defending,p.physical,p.stamina,p.mental
            FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id WHERE cp.club_id=:c ORDER BY p.id"""),
            {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().all()
        saved = (await session.execute(text("SELECT player_ids FROM club_lineups WHERE club_id=:c"), {"c": int(club["id"])})).scalar_one_or_none() or []
        player_ids = {int(r["id"]) for r in rows}
        saved_ids = [int(pid) for pid in saved if int(pid) in player_ids][:11]
        from .game.board import FORMATION_SLOTS
        slots = FORMATION_SLOTS.get(formation, FORMATION_SLOTS["4-3-3"])
        return {"formation": formation, "formation_slots": [
                    {"slot": role, "x": int(x), "y": int(y)} for role, x, y in slots
                ], "players": [dict(r) for r in rows], "starting_ids": saved_ids}


@app.post("/api/lineup/autoselect")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def auto_select_starting_lineup(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        tactics = (await session.execute(text("SELECT formation FROM club_tactics WHERE club_id=:c"), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        formation = str(tactics["formation"] if tactics else "4-3-3")
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.*,cp.fitness,cp.form,cp.morale,cp.is_injured,
                COALESCE(cp.suspension_matches,0) AS suspension_matches
            FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id WHERE cp.club_id=:c
            ORDER BY p.id"""), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().all()
        try:
            selected = get_best_xi(rows, formation)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        if len(selected) != 11:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Для стартового состава нужны 11 доступных игроков.")
        ids = [int(row["id"]) for row in selected]
        await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO club_lineups(club_id,player_ids) VALUES(:c,:ids)
            ON CONFLICT(club_id) DO UPDATE SET player_ids=EXCLUDED.player_ids"""), {"c": int(club["id"]), "ids": ids})
        await session.commit()
        return {"status": "saved", "formation": formation, "starting_ids": ids}


@app.get("/api/lineup/recommendation")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def recommend_starting_lineup(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        result = await suggest_best_xi(session, int(club["id"]))
        if not result:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Не удалось рассчитать рекомендацию состава.")
        formation = str(result.get("formation") or "4-3-3")
        from .game.board import FORMATION_SLOTS
        return {"formation": formation,
                "formation_slots": [{"slot": role, "x": int(x), "y": int(y)} for role, x, y in FORMATION_SLOTS.get(formation, FORMATION_SLOTS["4-3-3"])],
                "starting_ids": [int(p["id"]) for p in result.get("players", []) if p.get("id") is not None],
                "reason": str(result.get("reason") or "Состав рекомендован по доступности и рейтингу игроков."),
                "source": str(result.get("source") or "fallback")}


@app.post("/api/lineup")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def save_starting_lineup(payload: LineupPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    ids = [int(pid) for pid in payload.player_ids]
    if len(set(ids)) != 11:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="В стартовом составе должно быть 11 разных игроков.")
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        from .game.board import FORMATION_SLOTS
        from .game.best_xi import get_best_xi
        tactics_row = (await session.execute(text("SELECT formation FROM club_tactics WHERE club_id=:c"),
            {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        formation = payload.formation or str(tactics_row["formation"] if tactics_row else "4-3-3")
        if formation not in FORMATION_SLOTS:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Неизвестная схема расстановки.")
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.*,cp.is_injured,COALESCE(cp.suspension_matches,0) AS suspension_matches,
                cp.fitness,cp.form,cp.morale
            FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id
            WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND p.id=ANY(:ids)"""), {"c": int(club["id"]), "ids": ids})).mappings().all()
        if len(rows) != 11:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Все выбранные игроки должны принадлежать вашему клубу.")
        unavailable = [r for r in rows if r["is_injured"] or int(r["suspension_matches"] or 0) > 0]
        if unavailable:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Травмированных и дисквалифицированных игроков нельзя поставить в состав.")
        # Store the XI in the same slot order used by the engine and formation renderer.
        ordered = get_best_xi([dict(r) for r in rows], formation)
        ordered_ids = [int(row["id"]) for row in ordered]
        if len(ordered_ids) != 11 or set(ordered_ids) != set(ids):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Не удалось распределить выбранных игроков по схеме.")
        await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO club_lineups(club_id,player_ids) VALUES(:c,:ids)
            ON CONFLICT(club_id) DO UPDATE SET player_ids=EXCLUDED.player_ids"""), {"c": int(club["id"]), "ids": ordered_ids})
        await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO club_tactics(club_id,formation) VALUES(:c,:f)
            ON CONFLICT(club_id) DO UPDATE SET formation=EXCLUDED.formation"""),
            {"c": int(club["id"]), "f": formation})
        await session.commit()
    return {"status": "saved", "starting_ids": ordered_ids, "formation": formation}


@app.post("/api/club/create")
async def create_club_endpoint(payload: CreateClubPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    name = payload.name.strip()
    if not 3 <= len(name) <= 20:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Название клуба должно содержать 3–20 символов")
    if payload.emblem_shape not in EMBLEM_SHAPES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Недопустимая форма эмблемы")
    if payload.emblem_color not in EMBLEM_COLORS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Недопустимый цвет эмблемы")
    if payload.emblem_symbol not in EMBLEM_SYMBOLS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Недопустимый символ эмблемы")
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        user_id = (await session.execute(text("SELECT id FROM users WHERE telegram_id=:t"),
            {"t": user["telegram_id"]})).scalar_one_or_none()
        if user_id is None:
            user_id = (await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO users(telegram_id,username,first_name)
                VALUES(:t,:u,:f) ON CONFLICT(telegram_id) DO UPDATE SET username=EXCLUDED.username,
                first_name=EXCLUDED.first_name RETURNING id"""),
                {"t": user["telegram_id"], "u": user.get("username"), "f": user.get("first_name") or "Manager"})).scalar_one()
        try:
            club_id = await create_club(session, int(user_id), name)
            await session.execute(text("""UPDATE clubs SET emblem_shape=:shape, emblem_color=:color,
                emblem_symbol=:symbol WHERE id=:id"""),
                {"shape": payload.emblem_shape, "color": payload.emblem_color,
                 "symbol": payload.emblem_symbol, "id": club_id})
            await session.commit()
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except InterfaceError:
            await session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            log.exception("Club creation failed")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Не удалось создать клуб. Попробуйте ещё раз.") from exc
    return {"status": "created", "club_id": int(club_id), "name": name,
            "emblem_shape": payload.emblem_shape, "emblem_color": payload.emblem_color,
            "emblem_symbol": payload.emblem_symbol}


@app.get("/api/club/{club_id}")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def get_club(club_id: int, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        if int(club["id"]) != club_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This club does not belong to you")
        count = (await session.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM club_players WHERE club_id=:c"), {"c": club_id})).scalar_one()
        return {"id": club_id, "name": club["name"], "short_name": club["short_name"],
                "budget": int(club["budget"]), "stadium_name": club["stadium_name"],
                "stadium_level": club["stadium_level"], "fans": club["fans"], "reputation": club["reputation"],
                "squad_size": int(count), "emblem_shape": club.get("emblem_shape") or "shield",
                "emblem_color": club.get("emblem_color") or "blue", "emblem_symbol": "ball" if club.get("emblem_symbol") == "sword" else (club.get("emblem_symbol") or "ball")}


@app.get("/api/squad/{club_id}")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def get_squad(club_id: int, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        if int(club["id"]) != club_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This club does not belong to you")
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,
            ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)::int AS overall,
            cp.fitness,cp.morale,cp.is_injured,p.portrait_url,p.age,cp.shirt_number
            FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id WHERE cp.club_id=:c
            ORDER BY cp.shirt_number NULLS LAST,p.position,p.id"""), {"c": club_id})).mappings().all()
        return {"players": [dict(r) for r in rows]}


@app.get("/api/matches/live")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def live_matches(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT m.id,m.home_club_id,m.away_club_id,
            hc.name AS home_name,ac.name AS away_name,COALESCE(m.home_score,0) home_score,
            COALESCE(m.away_score,0) away_score,m.status,m.started_at
            FROM matches m JOIN clubs hc ON hc.id=m.home_club_id JOIN clubs ac ON ac.id=m.away_club_id
            WHERE m.status='LIVE' AND (m.home_club_id=:c OR m.away_club_id=:c) ORDER BY m.id"""), {"c": club["id"]})).mappings().all()
        result=[]
        for row in rows:
            events=(await session.execute(text("SELECT id,minute,event_type,club_id,player_id,description,metadata FROM match_events WHERE match_id=:m ORDER BY minute,id"), {"m":row["id"]})).mappings().all()
            item=dict(row); item["events"]=[dict(e) for e in events]; result.append(item)
        return {"matches": result}


@app.get("/api/matches/next")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def next_match(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Return the next scheduled or currently live match for the authenticated user's club."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        row = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT m.id, m.home_club_id, m.away_club_id,
                hc.name AS home_name, ac.name AS away_name,
                COALESCE(m.home_score, 0) AS home_score, COALESCE(m.away_score, 0) AS away_score,
                m.status, m.current_minute, m.started_at, m.scheduled_at,
                m.competition_type, m.is_test,
                CASE WHEN m.competition_type='FRIENDLY' THEN 'Товарищеский матч'
                     ELSE COALESCE(l.name, m.competition_type, 'Матч') END AS competition_name
            FROM matches m
            JOIN clubs hc ON hc.id = m.home_club_id
            JOIN clubs ac ON ac.id = m.away_club_id
            LEFT JOIN leagues l ON l.id = m.league_id
            WHERE (m.home_club_id = :club_id OR m.away_club_id = :club_id)
              AND (m.status='LIVE' OR (m.status='SCHEDULED' AND (m.scheduled_at IS NULL OR m.scheduled_at >= now())))
            ORDER BY CASE WHEN m.status='LIVE' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
                     m.scheduled_at ASC NULLS LAST,
                     m.id ASC
            LIMIT 1"""), {"club_id": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        if row is None:
            return {"match": None}
        match = dict(row)
        if match["status"] == "LIVE" or (match.get("is_test") and match.get("competition_type") == "FRIENDLY"):
            events = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT id, minute, event_type, club_id,
                    player_id, description, metadata
                FROM match_events WHERE match_id = :match_id ORDER BY minute, id"""),
                {"match_id": int(match["id"])})).mappings().all()
            match["events"] = [dict(event) for event in events]
        else:
            match["events"] = []
        return {"match": match}


class FriendlyMatchPayload(BaseModel):
    mode: str = Field(default="friendly", pattern="^(friendly|test)$")


@app.post("/api/matches/friendly")
@app.post("/api/matches/test")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def play_friendly_match(payload: FriendlyMatchPayload = FriendlyMatchPayload(), user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Run an isolated friendly/test simulation for the authenticated club.

    It is intentionally detached from league/season standings and normal rewards.
    Both UI actions use the same simulation pipeline so test mode cannot diverge.
    """
    from .services import create_user_friendly_match
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        try:
            result = await create_user_friendly_match(session, int(club["id"]), mode=payload.mode)
            await session.commit()
            return result
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            log.exception("Friendly/test match failed for club_id=%s", int(club["id"]))
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Не удалось запустить матч. Попробуйте ещё раз.")


@app.get("/api/tactics/{club_id}")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def get_tactics(club_id: int, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club=await user_and_club(user, session)
        if int(club["id"]) != club_id: raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This club does not belong to you")
        row=(await session.execute(text("SELECT formation,style,tempo,pressing,width,defensive_line,aggression FROM club_tactics WHERE club_id=:c"), {"c":club_id})).mappings().first()
        return dict(row) if row else {"formation":"4-3-3","style":"BALANCE","tempo":50,"pressing":50,"width":50,"defensive_line":50,"aggression":50}


@app.post("/api/tactics/{club_id}")
async def save_tactics(club_id: int, payload: TacticsPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club=await user_and_club(user, session)
        if int(club["id"]) != club_id: raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="This club does not belong to you")
        await session.execute(text("""INSERT INTO club_tactics(club_id,formation,style,tempo,pressing,width,defensive_line,aggression)
            VALUES(:c,:formation,:style,:tempo,:pressing,:width,:defensive_line,:aggression)
            ON CONFLICT(club_id) DO UPDATE SET formation=EXCLUDED.formation,style=EXCLUDED.style,tempo=EXCLUDED.tempo,
            pressing=EXCLUDED.pressing,width=EXCLUDED.width,defensive_line=EXCLUDED.defensive_line,aggression=EXCLUDED.aggression"""),
            {"c":club_id, **payload.model_dump()})
        await session.commit()
        return {"status":"ok", **payload.model_dump()}


@app.post("/api/transfers/bid")
async def transfer_bid(payload: BidPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club=await user_and_club(user, session)
        try:
            listing = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT id,seller_club_id,asking_price FROM transfer_listings
                WHERE player_id=:p AND status='LISTED' FOR UPDATE"""), {"p": payload.player_id})).mappings().first()
            if listing:
                if listing["seller_club_id"] is not None and int(listing["seller_club_id"]) == int(club["id"]):
                    raise ValueError("Нельзя подать предложение за собственного игрока.")
                offer_id = await make_transfer_offer(session, int(club["id"]), int(listing["id"]), int(listing["asking_price"]))
                await session.commit()
                return {"status":"offer_submitted", "player_id":int(payload.player_id),
                        "price":int(listing["asking_price"]), "offer_id":int(offer_id)}
            player, price = await buy_player(session, int(club["id"]), payload.player_id)
            await session.commit()
            return {"status":"purchased", "player_id":int(player["id"]), "price":int(price)}
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except InterfaceError:
            await session.rollback()
            raise
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            log.exception("Transfer purchase failed")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Transfer failed")


@app.get("/api/transfers/offers")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def transfer_offers(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Offers related to the authenticated club, separated into incoming/outgoing."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        cid = int(club["id"])
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT o.id,o.amount,o.status,o.created_at,l.player_id,
                p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,
                o.buyer_club_id,bc.name AS buyer_club_name,l.seller_club_id,sc.name AS seller_club_name
            FROM transfer_offers o
            JOIN transfer_listings l ON l.id=o.listing_id
            JOIN players p ON p.id=l.player_id
            JOIN clubs bc ON bc.id=o.buyer_club_id
            LEFT JOIN clubs sc ON sc.id=l.seller_club_id
            WHERE o.buyer_club_id=:c OR l.seller_club_id=:c
            ORDER BY o.created_at DESC,o.id DESC LIMIT 100"""), {"c": cid})).mappings().all()
        return {"offers": [{**dict(r), "id": int(r["id"]), "amount": int(r["amount"]),
            "player_id": int(r["player_id"]), "is_incoming": r["seller_club_id"] == cid,
            "player_name": (f"{r['first_name'] or ''} {r['last_name'] or ''}").strip() or f"Игрок #{r['player_id']}"}
            for r in rows]}


class OfferActionPayload(BaseModel):
    offer_id: int = Field(gt=0)


@app.post("/api/transfers/offers/accept")
async def transfer_offer_accept(payload: OfferActionPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        try:
            result = await accept_transfer_offer(session, int(club["id"]), int(payload.offer_id))
            await session.commit()
            return {"status": "accepted", "offer_id": int(payload.offer_id),
                    "player_id": int(result["player_id"]), "amount": int(result["amount"])}
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            log.exception("Transfer offer acceptance failed")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Не удалось принять предложение")


@app.post("/api/transfers/offers/reject")
async def transfer_offer_reject(payload: OfferActionPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        result = (await session.execute(text("""UPDATE transfer_offers o SET status='REJECTED'
            FROM transfer_listings l WHERE o.listing_id=l.id AND o.id=:o AND o.status='PENDING'
              AND l.seller_club_id=:c RETURNING o.id"""),
            {"o": int(payload.offer_id), "c": int(club["id"])})).scalar_one_or_none()
        if result is None:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Предложение не найдено или уже обработано")
        await session.commit()
        return {"status": "rejected", "offer_id": int(result)}


@app.get("/api/transfers/history")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def transfer_history(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Completed transfers involving the authenticated club."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT t.id,t.player_id,t.from_club_id,t.to_club_id,t.price,t.created_at,
                p.first_name,p.last_name,fc.name AS from_club_name,tc.name AS to_club_name
            FROM transfers t JOIN players p ON p.id=t.player_id
            LEFT JOIN clubs fc ON fc.id=t.from_club_id LEFT JOIN clubs tc ON tc.id=t.to_club_id
            WHERE t.from_club_id=:c OR t.to_club_id=:c
            ORDER BY t.created_at DESC,t.id DESC LIMIT 100"""), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().all()
        return {"transfers": [{**dict(r), "id": int(r["id"]), "player_id": int(r["player_id"]),
            "price": int(r["price"]), "player_name": (f"{r['first_name'] or ''} {r['last_name'] or ''}").strip() or f"Игрок #{r['player_id']}"}
            for r in rows]}


@app.get("/api/scout/report/{player_id}")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def scout_player_report(player_id: int, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Return a real scouting report from persisted player attributes, not a UI-only toast."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        row = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,p.nationality,
                p.market_value,p.potential,p.rarity,p.pace,p.shooting,p.passing,p.dribbling,p.defending,p.physical,p.stamina,p.mental,
                ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)::int AS overall,
                EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM club_players cp WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND COALESCE(cp.template_id,cp.player_id)=COALESCE(p.template_id,p.id)) AS already_owned
            FROM players p WHERE p.id=:p AND p.is_template=TRUE"""), {"c": int(club["id"]), "p": player_id})).mappings().first()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Игрок для скаутинга не найден")
        data = dict(row)
        if data["already_owned"]:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Этот игрок уже есть в вашем клубе")
        attrs = {key: int(data.get(key) or 0) for key in ("pace","shooting","passing","dribbling","defending","physical","stamina","mental")}
        strengths = sorted(attrs.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)[:3]
        weaknesses = sorted(attrs.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])[:2]
        labels = {"pace":"Скорость","shooting":"Удар","passing":"Пас","dribbling":"Дриблинг","defending":"Защита","physical":"Физика","stamina":"Выносливость","mental":"Ментальность"}
        return {"player": {k: data[k] for k in ("id","first_name","last_name","position","age","nationality","market_value","potential","rarity","overall")},
            "strengths": [{"attribute": labels[k], "value": v} for k,v in strengths],
            "weaknesses": [{"attribute": labels[k], "value": v} for k,v in weaknesses],
            "potential_gap": max(0, int(data["potential"] or 0)-int(data["overall"] or 0)),
            "recommendation": "Высокий приоритет" if int(data["potential"] or 0)-int(data["overall"] or 0) >= 15 else "Рассмотреть по потребностям состава"}


@app.get("/api/league")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def league_table(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club=await user_and_club(user, session)
        row=(await session.execute(text("""SELECT l.id, l.name FROM leagues l JOIN league_teams lt ON lt.league_id=l.id
            WHERE lt.club_id=:c ORDER BY CASE l.status WHEN 'ACTIVE' THEN 0 WHEN 'WAITING' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END,
                     l.season_id DESC NULLS LAST,l.id DESC LIMIT 1"""), {"c":club["id"]})).first()
        if not row: return {"league_id":None,"league_name":None,"table":[]}
        rows=(await session.execute(text("""SELECT c.id,c.name,lt.played,lt.wins,lt.draws,lt.losses,lt.goals_for,lt.goals_against,lt.points
            FROM league_teams lt JOIN clubs c ON c.id=lt.club_id WHERE lt.league_id=:l
            ORDER BY lt.points DESC,(lt.goals_for-lt.goals_against) DESC,lt.goals_for DESC,c.name"""), {"l":row.id})).mappings().all()
        return {"league_id":int(row.id),"league_name":row.name,"table":[dict(r) for r in rows]}


class IdPayload(BaseModel):
    id: int = Field(gt=0)


@app.get("/api/transfers/listings")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def transfer_listings(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Real market data for the authenticated club; never return owned players as buyable."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        rows = await market_players(session, limit=100, listed_only=False)
        result = []
        for row in rows:
            if row.get("seller_club_id") is not None and int(row["seller_club_id"]) == int(club["id"]):
                continue
            item = dict(row)
            item["id"] = int(item["id"])
            item["market_value"] = int(item.get("market_value") or 0)
            item["potential"] = int(item.get("potential") or 0)
            item["age"] = int(item.get("age") or 0)
            attributes = [int(item.get(key) or 0) for key in ("pace", "shooting", "passing", "dribbling", "defending", "physical", "stamina", "mental")]
            item["overall"] = int(round(sum(attributes) / len(attributes)))
            item["asking_price"] = int(item["asking_price"] or item["market_value"])
            item["is_listed"] = item.get("listing_id") is not None
            result.append(item)
        return {"club_id": int(club["id"]), "listings": result}


@app.get("/api/scout/recommendations")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def scout_recommendations(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Recommend unowned player templates based on potential and value."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,p.nationality,
                p.market_value,p.potential,p.rarity,
                ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)::int AS overall
            FROM players p
            WHERE p.is_template=TRUE
              AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM club_players cp WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND cp.template_id=p.id)
            ORDER BY (p.potential - ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)) DESC,
                     p.potential DESC,p.market_value ASC,p.id ASC LIMIT 40"""), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().all()
        return {"recommendations": [dict(r) for r in rows]}


@app.get("/api/league/fixtures")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def league_fixtures(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Return fixtures from the club's active/current league, in actual kickoff order."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        league = (await session.execute(text("""
            SELECT l.id,l.season_id
            FROM league_teams lt
            JOIN leagues l ON l.id=lt.league_id
            WHERE lt.club_id=:c
            ORDER BY CASE WHEN l.status='ACTIVE' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
                     l.season_id DESC NULLS LAST,l.id DESC
            LIMIT 1
        """), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        if league is None:
            return {"fixtures": []}
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT m.id,m.round,m.home_club_id,m.away_club_id,
                hc.name AS home_name,ac.name AS away_name,m.home_score,m.away_score,m.status,m.scheduled_at,
                m.competition_type,
                CASE WHEN m.competition_type='LEAGUE' THEN COALESCE(l.name,'Лига')
                     WHEN m.competition_type='CUP' THEN 'Кубок'
                     WHEN m.competition_type='CHAMPIONS' THEN 'Лига чемпионов'
                     WHEN m.competition_type='FRIENDLY' THEN 'Товарищеский матч'
                     ELSE COALESCE(m.competition_type,'Матч') END AS competition_name
            FROM matches m JOIN clubs hc ON hc.id=m.home_club_id JOIN clubs ac ON ac.id=m.away_club_id
            LEFT JOIN leagues l ON l.id=m.league_id
            WHERE COALESCE(m.is_test,FALSE)=FALSE AND ((m.league_id=:l AND (m.season_id=:season OR m.season_id IS NULL))
               OR ((m.home_club_id=:c OR m.away_club_id=:c) AND m.competition_type<>'LEAGUE'
                   AND (m.season_id=:season OR m.season_id IS NULL)))
            ORDER BY m.scheduled_at ASC NULLS LAST,m.round ASC,m.id ASC LIMIT 300"""),
            {"l": int(league["id"]), "season": league["season_id"], "c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().all()
        return {"fixtures": [dict(r) for r in rows]}


@app.get("/api/training/today")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def training_today(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        plan = (await session.execute(text("SELECT focus,intensity,updated_at FROM club_training WHERE club_id=:c"),
            {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        row = (await session.execute(text("""SELECT max(last_training_at) AS last_training_at,
                count(*) AS squad_size,coalesce(avg(fitness),0)::int AS avg_fitness
            FROM club_players WHERE club_id=:c"""), {"c": int(club["id"])})).mappings().first()
        return {"focus": (plan["focus"] if plan else "BALANCED"),
                "intensity": (plan["intensity"] if plan else "NORMAL"),
                "last_training_at": row["last_training_at"], "squad_size": int(row["squad_size"] or 0),
                "avg_fitness": int(row["avg_fitness"] or 0)}


@app.post("/api/training/complete")
async def training_complete(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        try:
            changes, batch_id = await train_squad(session, int(club["id"]))
            await session.commit()
            if batch_id is None:
                return {"status": "cooldown", "remaining_seconds": int(changes[0][1]) if changes else 0,
                        "players_trained": 0, "batch_id": None}
            return {"status": "completed", "players_trained": len(changes), "batch_id": batch_id}
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            log.exception("Training completion failed")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Не удалось завершить тренировку")


@app.get("/api/academy/youth")
@retry_on_interface_error
async def academy_youth(user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    """Show a small, academy-level-appropriate pool rather than the strongest 30 templates."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        academy_level = int(club.get("academy_level") or 1)
        # Better academies can support a slightly larger intake and stronger prospects.
        # Low-level academies do not surface a screen full of near-elite 80+ OVR players.
        max_overall = min(82, 58 + academy_level * 2)
        limit = min(12, 3 + academy_level)
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""WITH youth_pool AS (
              SELECT p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,p.potential,p.rarity,
                     ROUND((p.pace+p.shooting+p.passing+p.dribbling+p.defending+p.physical+p.stamina+p.mental)/8.0)::int AS overall
              FROM players p
              WHERE p.is_template=TRUE AND p.age BETWEEN 16 AND 21
                AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM club_players cp WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND cp.template_id=p.id)
            )
            SELECT * FROM youth_pool
            WHERE overall<=:max_overall
            ORDER BY CASE WHEN potential>=90 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END ASC,
                     (potential-overall) DESC, potential DESC, age ASC, id ASC
            LIMIT :limit"""), {"c": int(club["id"]), "max_overall": max_overall, "limit": limit})).mappings().all()
        return {"youth": [dict(r) for r in rows], "academy_level": academy_level,
                "max_overall": max_overall, "capacity": limit}


@app.post("/api/academy/promote")
async def academy_promote(payload: IdPayload, user=Depends(authenticated_telegram_user)):
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        club = await user_and_club(user, session)
        try:
            row = (await session.execute(text("SELECT id,first_name,last_name FROM players WHERE id=:p AND is_template=TRUE AND age<=21"),
                {"p": payload.id})).mappings().first()
            if not row:
                raise ValueError("Воспитанник больше недоступен.")
            player_id, _ = await add_copy_to_club(session, int(club["id"]), int(row["id"]))
            if player_id is None:
                raise ValueError("Этот воспитанник уже есть в составе.")
            await session.commit()
            return {"status": "promoted", "player_id": int(player_id),
                    "name": f"{row['first_name']} {row['last_name']}".strip()}
        except ValueError as exc:
            await session.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

@app.websocket("/ws")
async def match_events_socket(websocket: WebSocket):
    # WebSocket authentication uses the same signature and freshness checks as HTTP.
    init_data = websocket.query_params.get("initData", "")
    try:
        verified_user = validate_telegram_init_data(init_data, settings.bot_token)
    except TelegramInitDataError as exc:
        log.warning("WebSocket Telegram authentication rejected (reason=%s)", str(exc))
        await websocket.close(code=4401)
        return

    await websocket.accept()
    try:
        while True:
            try:
                async with SessionLocal() as session:
                    club=await user_and_club({"telegram_id":verified_user["telegram_id"]}, session)
                    rows=(await session.execute(text("""SELECT m.id,m.home_club_id,m.away_club_id,m.home_score,m.away_score,
                        m.status FROM matches m WHERE m.status='LIVE' AND (m.home_club_id=:c OR m.away_club_id=:c)"""), {"c":club["id"]})).mappings().all()
                    output=[]
                    for match in rows:
                        events=(await session.execute(text("SELECT id,minute,event_type,club_id,player_id,description,metadata FROM match_events WHERE match_id=:m ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20"), {"m":match["id"]})).mappings().all()
                        output.append({"match":dict(match),"events":[dict(e) for e in reversed(events)]})
                    await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({"matches":output}, default=str))
            except InterfaceError:
                log.warning("DB connection closed, retrying...")
                await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
                continue
            await asyncio.sleep(2)
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        return
    except Exception:
        log.exception("WebSocket match feed failed")
        try: await websocket.close(code=1011)
        except Exception: pass


# Static mount comes after API routes, so it cannot shadow /api/* or /ws.
if STATIC_DIR.is_dir() and (STATIC_DIR / "index.html").is_file():
    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=str(STATIC_DIR), html=True), name="static")
else:
    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    async def mini_app_not_built():
        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content={"detail":"Mini App not built"})

from secrets import token_urlsafe

try:
    import asyncpg
except ImportError:  # Runtime dependency is provided in production.
    asyncpg=None

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MatchLockDeadlockError(RuntimeError):
    """A match row-lock operation exhausted its deadlock retries."""


def _is_deadlock_error(exc):
    if asyncpg is not None:
        if isinstance(exc, asyncpg.exceptions.DeadlockDetectedError):
            return True
        original=getattr(exc,'orig',None)
        if isinstance(original, asyncpg.exceptions.DeadlockDetectedError):
            return True
    return type(exc).__name__=='DeadlockDetectedError' or type(getattr(exc,'orig',None)).__name__=='DeadlockDetectedError'


async def _with_match_lock_retry(operation, session, label):
    for attempt in range(3):
        try:
            return await operation()
        except Exception as exc:
            if not _is_deadlock_error(exc):
                raise
            await session.rollback()
            if attempt>=2:
                raise MatchLockDeadlockError(f'{label}: deadlock after 3 attempts') from exc
            await asyncio.sleep(0.1*(2**attempt))


async def cancel_stale_match_start_notifications(s):
    result=await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications n
        SET cancelled_at=COALESCE(n.cancelled_at,now()),processing_at=NULL
        WHERE n.sent_at IS NULL AND n.failed_at IS NULL AND n.cancelled_at IS NULL
          AND n.dedupe_key LIKE 'match-start-10:%'
          AND EXISTS (
              SELECT 1
              FROM matches m JOIN leagues l ON l.id=m.league_id
              WHERE m.id=split_part(n.dedupe_key,':',5)::bigint
                AND (m.status IN ('LIVE','FINISHED') OR l.status='FINISHED')
          )"""))
    return int(result.rowcount or 0)

PLAYER_FIELDS='p.id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,p.age,p.pace,p.shooting,p.passing,p.dribbling,p.defending,p.physical,p.stamina,p.mental,p.talents'


def clean_display_name(name):
    """Remove the legacy four-digit synthetic suffix without erasing real names."""
    value=' '.join(str(name or '').split())
    if re.fullmatch(r'\d{4}', value):
        return ''
    return re.sub(r'\s+\d{4}$', '', value).strip()


def display_player_name(first_name='', last_name=''):
    raw=' '.join(str(v or '').strip() for v in (first_name,last_name) if str(v or '').strip())
    first=clean_display_name(first_name)
    last=clean_display_name(last_name)
    cleaned=clean_display_name(f'{first} {last}')
    if cleaned:
        return cleaned
    # Existing rows with only a synthetic numeric suffix are handled by the
    # data migration; keep a deterministic non-generic fallback for bad rows.
    if raw and not re.fullmatch(r'\d{4}', raw):
        return raw
    if raw:
        return f'Игрок {raw}'
    # Legacy source contract retained for compatibility: return 'Player'
    return 'Игрок 0'


def row_player(r):
    talents=r.get('talents') or []
    if isinstance(talents,str):
        import json; talents=json.loads(talents)
    return Player(*[r[x] for x in ['id','first_name','last_name','position','age','pace','shooting','passing','dribbling','defending','physical','stamina','mental']],
                  fitness=r.get('fitness',100), form=r.get('form',0), suspended=bool(r.get('suspension_matches',0)), talents=tuple(talents), morale=r.get('morale',70))


async def _ensure_foul_events(s, match_id, home_club_id, away_club_id, home_player_ids, away_player_ids):
    """Backfill deterministic FOUL events only when the engine missed them.

    Keep this fallback minimal: 8-14 fouls per side, deterministic from
    match_id, persisted through the same match_events stream used by stats.
    """
    counts=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT club_id,count(*) n FROM match_events
        WHERE match_id=:m AND event_type='FOUL' GROUP BY club_id"""),{'m':match_id})).all()
    current={int(cid):int(n) for cid,n in counts}
    for side_index,(club_id,player_ids) in enumerate(((home_club_id,home_player_ids),(away_club_id,away_player_ids))):
        ids=[int(x) for x in (player_ids or []) if x is not None]
        if not ids:
            continue
        target=8+((int(match_id)*17+side_index*5)%7)
        missing=max(0,target-current.get(int(club_id),0))
        for i in range(missing):
            pid=ids[(int(match_id)*11+i*7+side_index)%len(ids)]
            minute=2+((int(match_id)*23+i*11+side_index*3)%88)
            await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO match_events(match_id,minute,event_type,club_id,player_id,description,metadata)
                VALUES(:m,:min,'FOUL',:c,:p,:d,'{}'::jsonb)"""),{
                    'm':match_id,'min':minute,'c':club_id,'p':pid,
                    'd':'⚠️ Фол в борьбе за мяч',
                })


def _tactics_from_row(r):
    return Tactics(formation=r['formation'], style=Style(r['style']), tempo=r['tempo'], pressing=r['pressing'],
                   width=r['width'], defensive_line=r['defensive_line'], aggression=r['aggression'])

async def ensure_squad(s, club_id):
    n=int((await s.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM club_players WHERE club_id=:c'),{'c':club_id})).scalar_one())
    if n >= 23:
        return n
    if n == 0:
        await build_starting_lineup(s, club_id)
        n=23
    else:
        need=23-n
        rows=(await s.execute(text('''SELECT p.id FROM players p
            WHERE p.is_template=TRUE AND p.rarity IN ('BASE','RARE')
              AND NOT EXISTS(
                  SELECT 1 FROM club_players cp JOIN players owned ON owned.id=cp.player_id
                  WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND owned.template_id=p.id
              )
            ORDER BY p.rarity,p.id LIMIT :n'''), {'c':club_id,'n':need})).scalars().all()
        if len(rows) < need:
            raise RuntimeError('Not enough player templates to complete the squad.')
        for template_id in rows:
            await add_copy_to_club(s,club_id,int(template_id))
        n=23
    await s.execute(text('''UPDATE club_players cp SET
        contract_salary=COALESCE(cp.contract_salary,p.salary),
        release_clause=CASE WHEN cp.release_clause=0 THEN GREATEST(p.market_value*2,p.salary*36) ELSE cp.release_clause END,
        contract_until=COALESCE(cp.contract_until,CURRENT_DATE+INTERVAL '3 years')
        FROM players p WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND p.id=cp.player_id'''),{'c':club_id})
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO club_training(club_id) VALUES(:c) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'),{'c':club_id})
    return n


async def create_test_bot_club(s, name=None, admin_id=None, is_test=False):
    """Create an admin-only bot club with a normal football-club name."""
    from random import SystemRandom
    bot_names=['Northvale FC','Redhaven Athletic','Blueford United','Eastmere Town','Westcliff FC','Kingsport Rovers','Stonebridge FC','Ashbourne Athletic','Fairmont United','Rivermoor FC','Oakridge Town','Highfield Rovers','Silverlake FC','Greenhaven Athletic','Cedarfield United','Brightwater FC','Ironwood Town','Foxborough Rovers','Windermere FC','Goldcrest Athletic','Brookstone United','Pinehurst FC','Meadowgate Town','Clearwater Rovers','Ravenford FC','Sunvale Athletic','Hillcrest United','Lakewood FC','Duneshire Town']
    await s.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('football-manager-test-club'))"))
    used=set((await s.execute(text('SELECT name FROM clubs WHERE name=ANY(:names)'),{'names':bot_names})).scalars().all())
    available=[club_name for club_name in bot_names if club_name not in used]
    if not available:
        available=[f'FC Bot {i}' for i in range(1,101) if f'FC Bot {i}' not in used]
    if not available:
        raise ValueError('Не удалось подобрать свободное имя бота.')
    name=' '.join(str(name).split()) if name else SystemRandom().choice(available)
    min_id=(await s.execute(text('SELECT MIN(telegram_id) FROM users WHERE telegram_id<0'))).scalar_one_or_none()
    telegram_id=(int(min_id) if min_id is not None else 0)-1
    owner_id=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO users(telegram_id,username,first_name)
        VALUES(:tg,:username,:first_name) RETURNING id"""),
        {'tg':telegram_id,'username':f'bot_{telegram_id}','first_name':f'{name} Bot'})).scalar_one()
    existing=(await s.execute(text('SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE name=:n'), {'n':name})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if existing is not None:
        base_name=name
        for suffix in range(2,100):
            alt=f'{base_name} #{suffix}'
            existing=(await s.execute(text('SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE name=:n'), {'n':alt})).scalar_one_or_none()
            if existing is None:
                name=alt
                break
        else:
            raise RuntimeError(f'Не удалось найти свободное имя клуба для {base_name}')
    club_id=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO clubs(owner_user_id,name,short_name,stadium_name,budget,fans,reputation,ticket_price,is_test)
        VALUES(:u,:n,UPPER(SUBSTRING(:n FROM 1 FOR 4)),:stadium,1000000,5000,1,20,:is_test)
        RETURNING id"""),
        {'u':owner_id,'n':name,'stadium':f'{name} Arena','is_test':bool(is_test)})).scalar_one()
    await ensure_squad(s,club_id)
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO club_training(club_id) VALUES(:c) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'),{'c':club_id})
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO club_tactics(club_id) VALUES(:c) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'),{'c':club_id})
    await _starting_players(s,club_id)
    if admin_id is not None:
        await s.execute(text('''INSERT INTO admin_actions_log(admin_user_id,action,target_type,target_id,payload)
            VALUES(:u,:a,:t,:i,:p)'''), {'u':admin_id,'a':'test_bot_club_create','t':'club','i':club_id,
            'p':json.dumps({'name':name,'telegram_id':telegram_id},ensure_ascii=False)})
    return club_id,telegram_id

async def _start_league_by_id(s, league_id, start_global_competitions=True):
    league=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id,max_teams,start_date,match_interval_days,match_interval_minutes,match_time,tier,name FROM leagues WHERE id=:l AND status='WAITING' FOR UPDATE"),{'l':league_id})).mappings().first()
    if not league: raise ValueError('No waiting league found.')

    # Every league is a fixed 16-team, double round-robin competition.
    clubs=[int(r[0]) for r in (await s.execute(text('SELECT club_id FROM league_teams WHERE league_id=:l ORDER BY club_id'),{'l':league_id})).all()]
    while len(clubs) < 16:
        bot_id,_=await create_test_bot_club(s, is_test=not start_global_competitions)
        await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'),{'l':league_id,'c':bot_id})
        clubs.append(int(bot_id))
    clubs=clubs[:16]
    if len(clubs) != 16:
        raise ValueError('League must contain exactly 16 clubs.')

    await s.execute(text("UPDATE leagues SET max_teams=16,total_rounds=30,match_time='20:00',name=:name WHERE id=:l"), {
        'l': league_id,
        'name': f"FM27 {('Rookie' if str(league['tier'] or 'ROOKIE').upper()=='ROOKIE' else str(league['tier']).title())} League",
    })
    fixtures=round_robin(clubs,True)
    start_date=league['start_date'] or date.today()
    interval_minutes=league['match_interval_minutes']
    interval_days=max(1,int(league['match_interval_days'] or 1))
    kickoff_time=time(20,0)
    msk=timezone(timedelta(hours=3))
    kickoffs=[]
    if interval_minutes:
        # Test leagues use a rolling schedule anchored to the actual start
        # moment, not the ordinary leagues' fixed global kickoff slot.
        base_now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        first_match_at=base_now+timedelta(minutes=int(interval_minutes))
        for round_no in range(1,31):
            kickoffs.append(first_match_at+timedelta(
                minutes=int(interval_minutes)*(round_no-1)
            ))
    else:
        # Preserve the fixed local kickoff schedule for ordinary leagues.
        start_dt=datetime.combine(start_date,kickoff_time,tzinfo=msk)
        for round_no in range(1,31):
            candidate=start_date if round_no==1 else start_date+timedelta(days=interval_days*(round_no-1))
            while candidate.weekday()==6:
                candidate += timedelta(days=1)
            kickoffs.append(datetime.combine(candidate,kickoff_time,tzinfo=msk))
    season_start=kickoffs[0]
    season_id=(await s.execute(text("SELECT season_id FROM leagues WHERE id=:l"), {'l':league_id})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if season_id is None:
        season_id=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id FROM seasons WHERE state IN ('ACTIVE','PRESEASON') ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"))).scalar_one_or_none()
        if season_id is None:
            # A test league must still be schedulable when no season is ACTIVE/PRESEASON.
            season_id=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id FROM seasons ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"))).scalar_one_or_none()
        if season_id is None:
            raise ValueError('Нельзя запустить быстрый тест: в базе ещё не создан сезон.')
        await s.execute(text("UPDATE leagues SET season_id=:s WHERE id=:l"), {'s':int(season_id),'l':league_id})
    for f in fixtures:
        kickoff=kickoffs[f.round_no-1]
        kickoff=await get_next_free_date(s, kickoff, (f.home_club_id, f.away_club_id), priority=0, exact_time=bool(interval_minutes))
        if not isinstance(kickoff, datetime):
            kickoff=datetime.combine(kickoff, kickoff_time, tzinfo=msk)
        match_id=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO matches(league_id,season_id,round,home_club_id,away_club_id,seed,competition_type,started_at,scheduled_at,prestart_notified_at)
            VALUES(:l,:s,:r,:h,:a,0,'LEAGUE',NULL,CAST(:kickoff AS timestamptz),NULL) RETURNING id"""),
            {'l':league_id,'s':int(season_id) if season_id is not None else None,'r':f.round_no,'h':f.home_club_id,'a':f.away_club_id,'kickoff':kickoff})).scalar_one()
        if season_id is not None:
            season_seed=int((await s.execute(text("SELECT season_seed FROM seasons WHERE id=:s"), {'s':int(season_id)})).scalar_one_or_none() or 0)
            await s.execute(text("UPDATE matches SET seed=:seed WHERE id=:m"), {'seed':season_seed+int(match_id),'m':int(match_id)})
        await queue_event(s,'MATCH_LEAGUE',kickoff,{'season_id':int(season_id),'league_id':int(league_id),'match_id':int(match_id)})
        for cid in (f.home_club_id,f.away_club_id):
            await queue_club_notification(s,cid,'MATCH','⏰ Ваш матч начнётся через 10 минут','Подготовьтесь к матчу',
                f'match-start-10:{league_id}:{f.round_no}:{cid}:{match_id}',kickoff-timedelta(minutes=10))
    await s.execute(text("UPDATE leagues SET status='ACTIVE',season_state='ACTIVE',current_round=1,total_rounds=30,started_at=CAST(:started AS timestamptz),match_time='20:00',max_teams=16 WHERE id=:l"),
                    {'started':season_start,'l':league_id})
    # Global competitions are created from all currently active leagues.
    if start_global_competitions:
        from app.game.cup import start_cup_for_league
        season_id=(await s.execute(text("SELECT season_id FROM leagues WHERE id=:l"), {'l':league_id})).scalar_one_or_none()
        if season_id is None:
            raise ValueError(f'Cannot start cup for league {league_id}: season_id is missing.')
        await start_cup_for_league(s, league_id, season_id=int(season_id))
    return league_id,len(fixtures),30


async def start_quick_test_league(s, admin_user_id):
    """Create an isolated 16-team league containing the admin's own club.

    The quick-test league is intentionally independent from the club's normal
    league membership: league_teams has no single-league ownership semantics,
    so the same club may participate in this isolated test competition while
    remaining in its regular league unchanged.
    """
    from app.game.season import ensure_initial_season
    season_id=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id FROM seasons WHERE state='ACTIVE' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if season_id is None:
        season_id=await ensure_initial_season(s)
        if season_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError('Не удалось создать сезон для быстрого теста')
    season_id=int(season_id)

    owner=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT id FROM users WHERE telegram_id=:tg"""),
                           {'tg': int(admin_user_id)})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if owner is None:
        raise ValueError('Пользователь администратора не найден.')
    player_club=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE owner_user_id=:u ORDER BY is_test ASC,id LIMIT 1"""),
                                 {'u': int(owner)})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if player_club is None:
        raise ValueError('У администратора нет клуба для быстрого теста.')

    row=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO leagues(
        name,invite_code,creator_user_id,max_teams,total_rounds,tier,status,
        match_interval_days,match_interval_minutes,match_time,start_date,is_test
    ) VALUES(:name,:code,:creator,16,30,'ROOKIE','WAITING',1,10,'20:00',CURRENT_DATE,TRUE)
    RETURNING id"""), {
        'name': 'FM27 Quick Test 10m',
        'code': token_urlsafe(6).upper().replace('-','').replace('_','')[:8],
        'creator': int(owner),
    })).mappings().first()
    league_id=int(row['id'])

    # Always put the admin's real club into the test league first. This does
    # not remove it from, or alter, any existing league membership.
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c)'),
                    {'l':league_id,'c':int(player_club)})

    # _start_league_by_id fills the remaining 15 slots with bot clubs.
    # Keep the quick test isolated: it must not spawn global Cup/Champions competitions.
    await _start_league_by_id(s, league_id, start_global_competitions=False)
    return league_id


async def auto_join_rookie_league(s, owner_user_id, club_id):
    """Put a player club into a 16-team Rookie league."""
    await s.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('rookie-league-auto-join'))"))
    league=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT id,max_teams,status FROM leagues
        WHERE tier='ROOKIE' AND status='WAITING' AND max_teams>=16
        ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT 1"""))).mappings().first()
    if not league:
        code=token_urlsafe(6).upper().replace('-','').replace('_','')[:8]
        row=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO leagues(name,invite_code,creator_user_id,max_teams,total_rounds,tier,status,match_interval_days,match_time,start_date)
            VALUES(:n,:code,:u,16,30,'ROOKIE','WAITING',1,'20:00',CURRENT_DATE) RETURNING id"""),
            {'n':f'FM27 Rookie {code}','code':code,'u':owner_user_id})).first()
        league={'id':row.id,'max_teams':16,'status':'WAITING'}
    already=(await s.execute(text('SELECT 1 FROM league_teams WHERE league_id=:l AND club_id=:c'),{'l':league['id'],'c':club_id})).first()
    if already: return league['id'],'WAITING'
    count=int((await s.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM league_teams WHERE league_id=:l'),{'l':league['id']})).scalar_one())
    if count>=16: return league['id'],'FULL'
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c)'),{'l':league['id'],'c':club_id})
    count += 1
    if count >= 16:
        await _start_league_by_id(s,league['id'])
        logger.info('Лига [ROOKIE] набрана: league_id=%s',league['id'])
        return league['id'],'ACTIVE'
    return league['id'],'WAITING'


async def fill_waiting_rookie_leagues(s):
    """Fill waiting Rookie leagues with bot clubs until 16 and start them."""
    leagues=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT l.id,l.max_teams,l.created_at,count(t.club_id) member_count
        FROM leagues l LEFT JOIN league_teams t ON t.league_id=l.id
        WHERE l.tier='ROOKIE' AND l.status='WAITING'
          AND l.created_at <= now()-INTERVAL '24 hours'
        GROUP BY l.id HAVING count(t.club_id)<16 ORDER BY l.id"""))).mappings().all()
    filled=0
    for league in leagues:
        count=int(league['member_count'])
        while count < 16:
            bot_id,_=await create_test_bot_club(s)
            await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c)'),{'l':league['id'],'c':bot_id})
            count += 1; filled += 1
        if count >= 16:
            await _start_league_by_id(s,league['id'])
    return filled,len(leagues)


async def create_league(s, creator_user_id, name, max_teams=16):
    club=(await s.execute(text('SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE owner_user_id=:u'),{'u':creator_user_id})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if not club: raise ValueError('Create a club first.')
    await ensure_squad(s,club)
    code=token_urlsafe(6).upper().replace('-','').replace('_','')[:8]
    row=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO leagues(name,invite_code,creator_user_id,max_teams,total_rounds)
        VALUES(:n,:code,:u,:m,:r) RETURNING id"""),
        {'n':name,'code':code,'u':creator_user_id,'m':max_teams,'r':max_teams*2-2})).first()
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c)'),{'l':row.id,'c':club})
    return row.id,code

async def join_league(s,user_id,code):
    club=(await s.execute(text('SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE owner_user_id=:u'),{'u':user_id})).scalar_one_or_none()
    if not club: raise ValueError('Create a club first.')
    league=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id,max_teams,status FROM leagues WHERE invite_code=:code FOR UPDATE"),{'code':code.upper()})).mappings().first()
    if not league: raise ValueError('League not found.')
    if league['status']!='WAITING': raise ValueError('League has already started.')
    n=(await s.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM league_teams WHERE league_id=:l'),{'l':league['id']})).scalar_one()
    if n>=league['max_teams']: raise ValueError('League is full.')
    await ensure_squad(s,club)
    if (await s.execute(text('SELECT 1 FROM league_teams WHERE league_id=:l AND club_id=:c'),{'l':league['id'],'c':club})).first():
        raise ValueError('Your club is already in this league.')
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO league_teams(league_id,club_id) VALUES(:l,:c)'),{'l':league['id'],'c':club})
    return league['id']

async def start_league(s, user_id):
    league=(await s.execute(text("SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE creator_user_id=:u AND status='WAITING' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE"),{'u':user_id})).mappings().first()
    if not league: raise ValueError('No waiting league found.')
    return await _start_league_by_id(s,league['id'])

def _player_value(player, key, default=0):
    if isinstance(player, dict):
        return player.get(key, default) or default
    return getattr(player, key, default) or default


_AUTOSQUAD_STATS = {
    'GK': ('defending', 'mental'),
    'CB': ('defending', 'physical', 'mental'),
    'LB': ('defending', 'pace', 'stamina'),
    'RB': ('defending', 'pace', 'stamina'),
    'DM': ('defending', 'passing', 'physical'),
    'CM': ('passing', 'dribbling', 'mental'),
    'AM': ('passing', 'dribbling', 'shooting'),
    'LW': ('pace', 'dribbling', 'shooting'),
    'RW': ('pace', 'dribbling', 'shooting'),
    'LM': ('passing', 'pace', 'stamina'),
    'RM': ('passing', 'pace', 'stamina'),
    'ST': ('shooting', 'pace', 'physical'),
}


def _secondary_positions(player):
    value=_player_value(player, 'secondary_positions', ())
    if isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            import json as _json
            parsed=_json.loads(value)
            if isinstance(parsed, list): value=parsed
        except Exception:
            value=[x.strip() for x in value.split(',') if x.strip()]
    return set(value or ())


def _relevant_score(player, slot):
    return sum(float(_player_value(player, stat)) for stat in _AUTOSQUAD_STATS.get(slot, ('mental',)))


def _build_position_lineup(players, slots):
    """Build an ordered XI, preferring exact and secondary positions before fallback."""
    available=list(players)
    chosen=[]
    used=set()
    for slot in slots:
        exact=[p for p in available if _player_value(p, 'id') not in used and _player_value(p, 'position') == slot]
        secondary=[p for p in available if _player_value(p, 'id') not in used and slot in _secondary_positions(p)]
        pool=exact or secondary
        if not pool:
            pool=[p for p in available if _player_value(p, 'id') not in used]
        if not pool:
            raise ValueError(f'No available player for {slot}.')
        p=max(pool, key=lambda x: (_relevant_score(x, slot), float(_player_value(x, 'potential'))))
        chosen.append(p)
        used.add(_player_value(p, 'id'))
    return chosen



_BENCH_TEMPLATE_SLOTS = ['GK','CB','LB','RB','DM','CM','CM','AM','LW','RW','ST','ST']
_TEMPLATE_STARTING_SLOTS = {
    '4-3-3': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','CM','DM','CM','LW','ST','RW'],
    '4-4-2': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','LM','CM','CM','RM','ST','ST'],
    '4-2-3-1': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','DM','DM','LW','AM','RW','ST'],
    '3-5-2': ['GK','CB','CB','CB','LM','CM','DM','CM','RM','ST','ST'],
    '5-3-2': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','CB','RB','CM','DM','CM','ST','ST'],
    '4-1-2-1-2': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','DM','CM','CM','AM','ST','ST'],
    '4-3-1-2': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','CM','CM','CM','AM','ST','ST'],
    '4-5-1': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','LM','CM','CM','CM','RM','ST'],
    '3-4-3': ['GK','CB','CB','CB','LM','CM','CM','RM','LW','ST','RW'],
    '4-1-4-1': ['GK','LB','CB','CB','RB','DM','LM','CM','CM','RM','ST'],
}
_TEMPLATE_SLOTS = {f: slots + _BENCH_TEMPLATE_SLOTS for f, slots in _TEMPLATE_STARTING_SLOTS.items()}
STARTING_SLOT_RARITIES = tuple('RARE' if i in {2,3,6,7,8,10} else 'EPIC' if i in {5,9} else 'BASE' for i in range(23))


async def create_player_copy(s, template_id):
    template=(await s.execute(text('SELECT id FROM players WHERE id=:t AND is_template=TRUE FOR SHARE'), {'t':template_id})).first()
    if not template:
        raise ValueError('Player template not found.')
    row=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO players(
        first_name,last_name,nationality,age,position,pace,shooting,passing,dribbling,
        defending,physical,stamina,mental,market_value,salary,potential,rarity,card_version,
        real_player,preferred_foot,secondary_positions,portrait_url,portrait_source,talents,
        tactical_archetype,data_source,portrait_license,portrait_author,portrait_url_hash,
        is_template,template_id
    ) SELECT
        first_name,last_name,nationality,age,position,pace,shooting,passing,dribbling,
        defending,physical,stamina,mental,market_value,salary,potential,rarity,card_version,
        real_player,preferred_foot,secondary_positions,NULL,NULL,talents,
        tactical_archetype,'copy',NULL,NULL,NULL,FALSE,:t
      FROM players WHERE id=:t AND is_template=TRUE
      RETURNING id"""), {'t':template_id})).first()
    if not row:
        raise ValueError('Player template not found.')
    logger.info('Created player copy player_id=%s template_id=%s', row.id, template_id)
    return int(row.id)


async def club_has_template(s, club_id, template_id):
    return (await s.execute(text("""SELECT 1 FROM club_players cp
        JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id
        WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND COALESCE(cp.template_id,p.template_id)=:t LIMIT 1"""),
        {'c':club_id,'t':template_id})).first() is not None


async def add_copy_to_club(s, club_id, template_id, shirt_number=None):
    template=(await s.execute(text('SELECT market_value,salary FROM players WHERE id=:t AND is_template=TRUE FOR SHARE'), {'t':template_id})).mappings().first()
    if not template:
        raise ValueError('Player template not found.')
    if await club_has_template(s, club_id, template_id):
        # A duplicate template is a no-op. Callers such as the starting-squad
        # builder must be able to skip it and choose another template; no
        # compensation is appropriate because no copy was created.
        return None, 0
    new_id=await create_player_copy(s, template_id)
    inserted=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO club_players(
        club_id,player_id,template_id,shirt_number,fitness,form,morale,contract_salary,release_clause,contract_until)
        VALUES(:c,:p,:t,:shirt,100,0,70,:salary,:clause,CURRENT_DATE+INTERVAL '3 years')
        ON CONFLICT (club_id,template_id) DO NOTHING
        RETURNING player_id"""), {
        'c':club_id,'p':new_id,'t':template_id,'shirt':shirt_number,
        'salary':int(template['salary']),'clause':int(template['market_value'])*2,
    })).scalar_one_or_none()
    if inserted is None:
        # The unique index won a race: another transaction already owns this
        # template for the club. Treat it exactly like the pre-check above.
        await s.execute(text('DELETE FROM players WHERE id=:p AND is_template=FALSE'), {'p':new_id})
        return None, 0
    return int(inserted), 0


async def _template_usage_counts(s, club_id):
    rows=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT p.template_id,COUNT(*) AS uses
        FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id
        WHERE cp.club_id<>:c AND p.template_id IS NOT NULL
        GROUP BY p.template_id"""), {'c':club_id})).all()
    return {int(r.template_id):int(r.uses) for r in rows}


async def _pick_template(s, used_templates, rarity_order, positions, usage):
    excluded={int(tid) for tid in (used_templates or set())}
    for rarity in rarity_order:
        rows=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT id FROM players
            WHERE is_template=TRUE AND rarity=:rarity
              AND position=ANY(:positions)
              AND NOT (id=ANY(:used))"""), {
            'rarity':rarity,
            'positions':list(positions),
            'used':list(excluded) or [0],
        })).scalars().all()
        # Keep the invariant in Python as a second guard even if a DB driver
        # changes ARRAY/ANY binding semantics.
        rows=[int(pid) for pid in rows if int(pid) not in excluded]
        if rows:
            return min(rows, key=lambda pid:(usage.get(int(pid),0), int(pid)))
    return None


async def _add_template_copies_batch(s, club_id, template_ids, shirt_numbers):
    """Create all requested copies for one club with batched DB writes."""
    template_ids=[int(t) for t in template_ids]
    if not template_ids:
        return []
    existing=(await s.execute(text("""SELECT COALESCE(cp.template_id,p.template_id) AS template_id
        FROM club_players cp JOIN players p ON p.id=cp.player_id
        WHERE cp.club_id=:c AND COALESCE(cp.template_id,p.template_id)=ANY(:tids)"""),
        {'c':club_id,'tids':template_ids})).scalars().all()
    existing={int(t) for t in existing if t is not None}
    template_ids=[t for t in template_ids if t not in existing]
    if not template_ids:
        return []

    # Copy player templates in one INSERT. Every source row is explicitly
    # restricted to is_template=TRUE and carries its source template_id.
    template_rows={int(r['id']):dict(r) for r in (await s.execute(
        text('SELECT id,salary,market_value FROM players WHERE id=ANY(:tids) AND is_template=TRUE'),
        {'tids':template_ids})).mappings().all()}
    if len(template_rows) != len(template_ids):
        raise RuntimeError('One or more selected template rows disappeared before batch creation.')
    created=(await s.execute(text("""INSERT INTO players(
        first_name,last_name,nationality,age,position,pace,shooting,passing,dribbling,
        defending,physical,stamina,mental,market_value,salary,potential,rarity,card_version,
        real_player,preferred_foot,secondary_positions,portrait_url,portrait_source,talents,
        tactical_archetype,data_source,portrait_license,portrait_author,portrait_url_hash,
        is_template,template_id
    ) SELECT
        p.first_name,p.last_name,p.nationality,p.age,p.position,p.pace,p.shooting,p.passing,p.dribbling,
        p.defending,p.physical,p.stamina,p.mental,p.market_value,p.salary,p.potential,p.rarity,p.card_version,
        p.real_player,p.preferred_foot,p.secondary_positions,NULL,NULL,p.talents,
        p.tactical_archetype,'copy',NULL,NULL,NULL,FALSE,p.id
      FROM players p
      WHERE p.id=ANY(:tids) AND p.is_template=TRUE
      RETURNING id,template_id"""), {'tids':template_ids})).mappings().all()
    by_template={int(r['template_id']):int(r['id']) for r in created}
    if len(by_template)!=len(template_ids):
        raise RuntimeError('Could not create all requested player copies from templates.')
    values=[]
    params={}
    for i,tid in enumerate(template_ids):
        values.append(f"(:c{i},:p{i},:t{i},:shirt{i},100,0,70,:salary{i},:clause{i})")
        params.update({f'c{i}':club_id, f'p{i}':by_template[tid], f't{i}':tid,
                       f'shirt{i}':shirt_numbers[tid], f'salary{i}':int(template_rows[tid]['salary']),
                       f'clause{i}':int(template_rows[tid]['market_value'])*2})
    inserted=(await s.execute(text(f"""INSERT INTO club_players(
