                WHEN 'MATCH' THEN COALESCE(ns.match,TRUE)
                WHEN 'INJURY' THEN COALESCE(ns.injury,TRUE)
                WHEN 'TRANSFER' THEN COALESCE(ns.transfer,TRUE)
                WHEN 'FINANCE' THEN COALESCE(ns.finance,TRUE)
                WHEN 'DEVELOPMENT' THEN COALESCE(ns.development,TRUE)
                WHEN 'DISCIPLINE' THEN COALESCE(ns.discipline,TRUE)
                WHEN 'MORALE' THEN COALESCE(ns.morale,TRUE)
                WHEN 'TOURNAMENT' THEN COALESCE(ns.tournament,TRUE)
                ELSE FALSE
              END
        ORDER BY COALESCE(n.next_attempt_at,n.deliver_at),n.id"""), {'ids': ids})).mappings().all()

    row_ids={r['id'] for r in rows}
    if row_ids:
        await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications
            SET processing_at=now(),delivery_attempts=delivery_attempts+1
            WHERE id=ANY(:ids) AND sent_at IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL AND failed_at IS NULL"""), {'ids': list(row_ids)})
    disabled_ids=[i for i in ids if i not in row_ids]
    if disabled_ids:
        await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications SET processing_at=NULL
            WHERE id=ANY(:ids) AND sent_at IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL AND failed_at IS NULL"""), {'ids': disabled_ids})
    await s.commit()

    def _priority(row):
        key=str(row.get('dedupe_key') or '')
        category=str(row.get('category') or '')
        return key.startswith(('match-start-10:','match-started:','match-finished:','live-event:'))

    # Group non-critical notifications created in the same minute per user.
    # Match kickoff/start/end and GOAL/RED stay as separate messages.
    grouped=[]
    by_bucket=defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        created=row.get('created_at')
        bucket=created.replace(second=0,microsecond=0) if created is not None else None
        if _priority(row) or bucket is None:
            grouped.append((f"row:{row['id']}",[row]))
        else:
            by_bucket[(row['telegram_id'],bucket)].append(row)
    for key,bucket_rows in by_bucket.items():
        if len(bucket_rows) < 3:
            grouped.extend((f"row:{r['id']}",[r]) for r in bucket_rows)
        else:
            grouped.append((f"group:{key[0]}:{key[1].isoformat()}",bucket_rows))

    async def _send(group):
        rows_for_send=group
        row=rows_for_send[0]
        try:
            from aiogram.types import InlineKeyboardMarkup, InlineKeyboardButton
            markup=None
            if len(rows_for_send)==1:
                key=str(row.get('dedupe_key') or '')
                if key.startswith('match-start-10:') or key.startswith('match-started:'):
                    parts=key.split(':')
                    mid=parts[4] if key.startswith('match-start-10:') else parts[1]
                    markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[
                        [
                            InlineKeyboardButton(text='📺 Смотреть матч',callback_data=f'match:watch:{mid}'),
                            InlineKeyboardButton(text='🔍 Обзор матча',callback_data=f'match:preview:{mid}')
                        ],
                        [InlineKeyboardButton(text='🏠 Главное меню',callback_data='menu:main')]
                    ])
                elif key.startswith('live-event:') and row.get('title')=='⚽ ГОЛ':
                    event_id=key.split(':',1)[1]
                    match_id=(await s.execute(text('SELECT match_id FROM match_events WHERE id=:e'),{'e':event_id})).scalar_one_or_none()
                    if match_id:
                        markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[[
                            InlineKeyboardButton(text='📺 К матчу',callback_data=f'match:watch:{match_id}'),
                            InlineKeyboardButton(text='🏠 Главное меню',callback_data='menu:main')
                        ]])
                elif key.startswith('match-finished:'):
                    mid=key.split(':',1)[1]
                    markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[[InlineKeyboardButton(text='📊 Полный отчёт',callback_data=f'match:watch:report:{mid}')]])
                elif key.startswith('season-finished:'):
                    markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[[
                        InlineKeyboardButton(text='🏆 Таблица',callback_data='league:table'),
                        InlineKeyboardButton(text='🏠 Главное меню',callback_data='menu:main')
                    ]])
            if len(rows_for_send)==1:
                text_value=f"<b>{row['title']}</b>\n{str(row['body'] or '').replace(r'\n','\n')}"
            else:
                lines=['<b>📋 Уведомления:</b>']
                for item in rows_for_send:
                    body=str(item['body'] or '').replace(r'\n','\n').replace('\n',' ')
                    lines.append(f"• {item['title']}: {body}")
                text_value='\n'.join(lines)
            await telegram_rate_limiter.acquire()
            try:
                await bot.send_message(row['telegram_id'],text_value,reply_markup=markup)
            except TelegramRetryAfter as exc:
                await asyncio.sleep(float(exc.retry_after))
                await telegram_rate_limiter.acquire()
                await bot.send_message(row['telegram_id'],text_value,reply_markup=markup)
            except TelegramAPIError as exc:
                error_code=getattr(exc,'error_code',None)
                status_code=getattr(exc,'status_code',None)
                retry_after=getattr(exc,'retry_after',None)
                if error_code == 429 or status_code == 429 or retry_after is not None:
                    delay=float(retry_after or 1)
                    await asyncio.sleep(delay)
                    await telegram_rate_limiter.acquire()
                    await bot.send_message(row['telegram_id'],text_value,reply_markup=markup)
                else:
                    raise
            return [r['id'] for r in rows_for_send],None
        except Exception as exc:
            return [r['id'] for r in rows_for_send],exc

    # Telegram I/O is the expensive part. Run the claimed sends concurrently so
    # a burst of live events cannot serialize 20-50 network round trips.
    results=await asyncio.gather(*(_send(items) for _,items in grouped))
    delivered=0
    for row_ids,exc in results:
        for row_id in row_ids:
            if exc is not None:
                attempts=(await s.execute(text('SELECT delivery_attempts FROM notifications WHERE id=:id'), {'id':row_id})).scalar_one_or_none() or 1
                delay=min(300,2 ** min(int(attempts),8))
                if int(attempts) >= 8:
                    await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications
                        SET processing_at=NULL,failed_at=now(),next_attempt_at=NULL,last_error=:err
                        WHERE id=:id AND sent_at IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL"""), {'id':row_id,'err':str(exc)[:500]})
                else:
                    await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications
                        SET processing_at=NULL,next_attempt_at=now()+make_interval(secs=>:delay),last_error=:err
                        WHERE id=:id AND sent_at IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL AND failed_at IS NULL"""), {'id':row_id,'delay':delay,'err':str(exc)[:500]})
                continue
            await s.execute(text("""UPDATE notifications
                SET sent_at=now(),processing_at=NULL,last_error=NULL
                WHERE id=:id AND sent_at IS NULL"""), {'id':row_id})
            delivered+=1
    await s.commit()
    return delivered


# --- Notifications ---------------------------------------------------------
NOTIFICATION_CATEGORIES = {
    'MATCH': 'match', 'INJURY': 'injury', 'TRANSFER': 'transfer',
    'FINANCE': 'finance', 'DEVELOPMENT': 'development', 'DISCIPLINE': 'discipline',
    'MORALE': 'morale', 'TOURNAMENT': 'tournament',
}

async def queue_notification(s, user_id, category, title, body, dedupe_key=None, deliver_at=None):
    if category not in NOTIFICATION_CATEGORIES:
        raise ValueError('Unknown notification category.')
    pref=NOTIFICATION_CATEGORIES[category]
    await s.execute(text(f'''INSERT INTO notification_settings(user_id) VALUES(:u) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'''), {'u':user_id})
    enabled=(await s.execute(text(f'SELECT {pref} FROM notification_settings WHERE user_id=:u'), {'u':user_id})).scalar_one()
    if not enabled: return None
    row=(await s.execute(text('''INSERT INTO notifications(user_id,category,title,body,dedupe_key,deliver_at)
        VALUES(:u,:c,:t,:b,:d,COALESCE(CAST(:at AS timestamptz),now()))
        ON CONFLICT(user_id,dedupe_key) WHERE dedupe_key IS NOT NULL DO NOTHING
        RETURNING id'''), {'u':user_id,'c':category,'t':title,'b':body,'d':dedupe_key,'at':deliver_at})).first()
    return row.id if row else None

async def queue_club_notification(s, club_id, category, title, body, dedupe_key=None, deliver_at=None):
    owner=(await s.execute(text('''SELECT u.id,u.telegram_id FROM clubs c JOIN users u ON u.id=c.owner_user_id WHERE c.id=:c'''), {'c':club_id})).mappings().first()
    if not owner or int(owner['telegram_id']) < 0:
        return None
    return await queue_notification(s, owner['id'], category, title, body, dedupe_key, deliver_at)

async def notification_list(s, user_id, limit=20):
    return (await s.execute(text('''SELECT id,category,title,body,created_at,read_at
        FROM notifications WHERE user_id=:u AND deliver_at<=now() AND sent_at IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT :n'''), {'u':user_id,'n':limit})).mappings().all()

async def notification_unread_count(s, user_id):
    return int((await s.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM notifications WHERE user_id=:u AND read_at IS NULL AND deliver_at<=now() AND sent_at IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL'), {'u':user_id})).scalar_one())

async def mark_notifications_read(s, user_id):
    await s.execute(text('UPDATE notifications SET read_at=COALESCE(read_at,now()) WHERE user_id=:u AND read_at IS NULL AND deliver_at<=now() AND sent_at IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL'), {'u':user_id})

async def set_notification_preference(s, user_id, category, enabled):
    if category not in NOTIFICATION_CATEGORIES: raise ValueError('Unknown notification category.')
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO notification_settings(user_id) VALUES(:u) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'), {'u':user_id})
    col=NOTIFICATION_CATEGORIES[category]
    enabled=bool(enabled)
    await s.execute(text(f'UPDATE notification_settings SET {col}=:v WHERE user_id=:u'), {'v':enabled,'u':user_id})
    if not enabled:
        await s.execute(text('''UPDATE notifications SET cancelled_at=COALESCE(cancelled_at,now()),processing_at=NULL
            WHERE user_id=:u AND category=:c AND sent_at IS NULL AND failed_at IS NULL'''), {'u':user_id,'c':category})

async def notification_preferences(s, user_id):
    await s.execute(text('INSERT INTO notification_settings(user_id) VALUES(:u) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING'), {'u':user_id})
    return (await s.execute(text('SELECT * FROM notification_settings WHERE user_id=:u'), {'u':user_id})).mappings().first()

from app.game.rng import rng
from types import SimpleNamespace

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.game.ratings import player_rating
from app.services import add_copy_to_club, display_player_name, record_financial_transaction
from app.game.liveops import get_liveops_config, multiplier


async def list_packs(session):
    return (await session.execute(text("""
        SELECT id,name,price_coins,price_stars,min_ovr,max_ovr
        FROM packs ORDER BY id
    """))).mappings().all()


def _template_ovr(row):
    player = SimpleNamespace(
        id=int(row['id']), position=str(row['position']),
        pace=float(row['pace'] or 0), shooting=float(row['shooting'] or 0),
        passing=float(row['passing'] or 0), dribbling=float(row['dribbling'] or 0),
        defending=float(row['defending'] or 0), physical=float(row['physical'] or 0),
        stamina=float(row['stamina'] or 0), mental=float(row['mental'] or 0),
        fitness=100, form=0, morale=70,
    )
    return int(round(float(player_rating(player))))


async def open_pack(session, club_id, pack_id):
    pack = (await session.execute(text("""
        SELECT id,name,price_coins,price_stars,min_ovr,max_ovr
        FROM packs WHERE id=:p FOR UPDATE
    """), {'p': int(pack_id)})).mappings().first()
    if not pack:
        raise ValueError('Пак не найден.')

    # Lock the club through the financial transaction. The transaction helper
    # performs the budget check and records the debit atomically.
    candidates = (await session.execute(text("""
        SELECT id,first_name,last_name,nationality,age,position,pace,shooting,
               passing,dribbling,defending,physical,stamina,mental,market_value,
               salary,potential,rarity,card_version,real_player,preferred_foot,portrait_url,
               secondary_positions,talents,tactical_archetype
        FROM players
        WHERE is_template=TRUE
        ORDER BY id
    """))).mappings().all()
    matching = [r for r in candidates if int(pack['min_ovr']) <= _template_ovr(r) <= int(pack['max_ovr'])]
    if not matching:
        raise ValueError('В этом паке сейчас нет подходящих игроков.')

    season_row = (await session.execute(text("SELECT id,season_seed FROM seasons WHERE state='ACTIVE' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"))).mappings().first()
    root_seed = int(season_row['season_seed']) if season_row else int(pack['id'])
    rng(root_seed, 'pack_open', int(club_id), int(pack_id)).shuffle(matching)
    template = None
    for row in matching:
        owned = await session.execute(text("""
            SELECT 1 FROM club_players
            WHERE club_id=:c AND template_id=:t LIMIT 1
        """), {'c': int(club_id), 't': int(row['id'])})
        if owned.first() is None:
            template = row
            break
    if template is None:
        raise ValueError('Для этого пака не осталось свободных карточек в базе.')

    cfg = await get_liveops_config(session, int(season_row['id'])) if season_row else {}
    price = int(round(int(pack['price_coins']) * multiplier(cfg, 'pack_price_multiplier')))
    await record_financial_transaction(
        session, int(club_id), 'OTHER', -price,
        f'Открытие пака «{pack["name"]}»'
    )
    player_id, _ = await add_copy_to_club(session, int(club_id), int(template['id']))
    if player_id is None:
        raise ValueError('Не удалось выдать игрока клубу.')

    await session.execute(text("""
        INSERT INTO pack_history(club_id,pack_id,player_id,created_at)
        VALUES(:c,:p,:player,now())
    """), {'c': int(club_id), 'p': int(pack['id']), 'player': int(player_id)})

    return {
        'pack_id': int(pack['id']), 'pack_name': pack['name'],
        'player_id': int(player_id), 'template_id': int(template['id']),
        'name': display_player_name(template['first_name'], template['last_name']),
        'position': template['position'], 'ovr': _template_ovr(template),
        'rarity': str(template['rarity'] or 'BASE').upper(), 'price_coins': price,
        'age': int(template['age'] or 18), 'nationality': template['nationality'],
        'potential': int(template['potential'] or _template_ovr(template)),
        'portrait_url': template['portrait_url'], 'card_version': template['card_version'],
    }


async def send_stars_invoice(*args, **kwargs):
    """Telegram Stars invoice integration — в разработке."""
    return None

export const TELEGRAM_REQUIRED_MESSAGE = 'Откройте приложение из Telegram';

function initData(): string {
  const tg = (window as any).Telegram?.WebApp;
  return typeof tg?.initData === 'string' ? tg.initData.trim() : '';
}

export async function api<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const telegramInitData = initData();
  // Do not send unauthenticated API requests when opened in a normal browser.
  if (!telegramInitData) throw new Error(TELEGRAM_REQUIRED_MESSAGE);

  const headers = new Headers(options.headers);
  headers.set('Accept', 'application/json');
  headers.set('X-Telegram-Init-Data', telegramInitData);
  if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
  let response: Response;
  try { response = await fetch(path, { ...options, headers }); }
  catch { throw new Error('Не удалось связаться с сервером. Проверь интернет и попробуй снова.'); }
  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `Ошибка API (${response.status})`;
    try { const data = await response.json(); if (data?.detail) message = String(data.detail); } catch { /* non-JSON error */ }
    throw new Error(message);
  }
  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

type Page = 'home'|'team'|'tactics'|'squad'|'lineup'|'profile'|'match'|'transfers'|'training'|'league'|'stadium'|'academy'|'scout'|'packs';

const money = (n?: number | null) => n == null ? '—' : '€' + new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU').format(n);
const validDate = (value?: string | null) => { if (!value) return null; const d = new Date(value); return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d; };
const dateText = (value?: string | null) => { const d = validDate(value); return d ? d.toLocaleString('ru-RU',{day:'2-digit',month:'long',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) : 'Дата не назначена'; };
const dateKey = (value?: string | null) => { const d = validDate(value); return d ? `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}` : 'undated'; };
const errorText = (e: unknown) => e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось загрузить данные. Попробуйте ещё раз.';
const rarityLabel = (rarity?: string) => ({BASE:'ОБЫЧНАЯ',RARE:'РЕДКАЯ',EPIC:'ЭПИЧЕСКАЯ',LEGENDARY:'ЛЕГЕНДАРНАЯ'} as Record<string,string>)[String(rarity||'BASE').toUpperCase()] || 'ОБЫЧНАЯ';
const rarityClass = (rarity?: string) => ({BASE:'rarity-base',RARE:'rarity-rare',EPIC:'rarity-epic',LEGENDARY:'rarity-legendary'} as Record<string,string>)[String(rarity||'BASE').toUpperCase()] || 'rarity-base';
const positionFits: Record<string,string[]> = {GK:['GK'],LB:['LB','CB','LM','LW'],CB:['CB','LB','RB','DM'],RB:['RB','CB','RM','RW'],DM:['DM','CM','CB'],CM:['CM','DM','AM','LM','RM'],LM:['LM','LW','LB','CM'],RM:['RM','RW','RB','CM'],AM:['AM','CM','LW','RW','ST'],LW:['LW','LM','ST','AM'],RW:['RW','RM','ST','AM'],ST:['ST','LW','RW','AM']};
function orderIdsForSlots(ids:number[], players:LineupPlayer[], slots:FormationSlot[]) {
  const remaining = ids.map(id=>players.find(p=>p.id===id)).filter((p):p is LineupPlayer=>Boolean(p));
  return slots.map(slot=>{
    const candidates=remaining.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
      const fitA=(positionFits[slot.slot]||[slot.slot]).includes(a.position)?1:0;
      const fitB=(positionFits[slot.slot]||[slot.slot]).includes(b.position)?1:0;
      const exactA=a.position===slot.slot?1:0, exactB=b.position===slot.slot?1:0;
      return exactB-exactA || fitB-fitA || b.overall-a.overall;
    });
    const chosen=candidates[0];
    if(!chosen)return null;
    remaining.splice(remaining.findIndex(p=>p.id===chosen.id),1);
    return chosen.id;
  }).filter((id):id is number=>id!==null);
}

Цвет карточки соответствует редкости.</p><div className="pack-grid">{packs.map((p,i)=><Card key={p.id}><div className={'pack pack'+(i%4)}><span className="pack-glow">✦</span>{p.min_ovr>=85?'👑':p.min_ovr>=75?'💎':'🎴'}</div><b>{p.name}</b><small>OVR {p.min_ovr}–{p.max_ovr}</small><strong>{money(p.price_coins)}</strong><Button disabled={busyId===p.id||packOpening} onClick={async()=>{setBusyId(p.id);setError('');setPackOpening(true);setLastPackResult(null);try{const r=await openPack(p.id);setLastPackResult(r);await refreshClub();await new Promise(resolve=>window.setTimeout(resolve,1850));setPackOpening(false);}catch(e){setPackOpening(false);setError(errorText(e));}finally{setBusyId(null);}}}>{busyId===p.id?'Открываем…':'Открыть пак'}</Button></Card>)}</div>{lastPackResult&&!packOpening&&<Card cls="pack-result-wrap"><h3>Новая карточка</h3><div className={'player-card '+rarityClass(lastPackResult.rarity)}><div className="player-card-top"><span>{rarityLabel(lastPackResult.rarity)}</span><strong>OVR {lastPackResult.ovr}</strong></div><div className="player-card-art">{lastPackResult.portrait_url&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(lastPackResult.portrait_url)?<img src={lastPackResult.portrait_url} alt={lastPackResult.name}/>:<span>👤</span>}<b>{lastPackResult.position}</b></div><div className="player-card-info"><h2>{lastPackResult.name}</h2><p>{lastPackResult.age||'—'} лет · {lastPackResult.nationality||'Национальность не указана'}</p><div className="player-card-stats"><span><small>ОВР</small><b>{lastPackResult.ovr}</b></span><span><small>ПОТЕНЦИАЛ</small><b>{lastPackResult.potential??'—'}</b></span><span><small>РЕДКОСТЬ</small><b>{rarityLabel(lastPackResult.rarity)}</b></span></div></div></div><p className="muted">Игрок добавлен в твой клуб.</p></Card>}{!packs.length&&<p className="muted">{loading?'Загрузка паков…':'Паки пока недоступны.'}</p>}{packOpening&&<div className="pack-opening-overlay"><div className="pack-opening-content"><div className="pack-opening-back"><span>FM</span><b>PLAYER PACK</b><i>✦</i></div><p>{lastPackResult?'Карточка открывается…':'Пак вскрывается…'}</p>{lastPackResult&&<div className={'player-card reveal-card '+rarityClass(lastPackResult.rarity)}><div className="player-card-top"><span>{rarityLabel(lastPackResult.rarity)}</span><strong>OVR {lastPackResult.ovr}</strong></div><div className="player-card-art">{lastPackResult.portrait_url&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(lastPackResult.portrait_url)?<img src={lastPackResult.portrait_url} alt={lastPackResult.name}/>:<span>👤</span>}<b>{lastPackResult.position}</b></div><div className="player-card-info"><h2>{lastPackResult.name}</h2><p>{lastPackResult.age||'—'} лет</p></div></div>}</div></div>}</>}
    {page==='stadium'&&<><Header title="Стадион"/><Card><div className="stadium-art">🏟️</div><h2>{stadium?.name||club.stadium_name||'Стадион клуба'}</h2>{stadium?<><div className="teamstats"><div><small>Уровень</small><b>{stadium.level}/20</b></div><div><small>Вместимость</small><b>{new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU').format(stadium.capacity)}</b></div><div><small>Болельщики</small><b>{new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU').format(stadium.fans)}</b></div></div><div className="effect"><span>Цена билета</span><b>{money(stadium.ticket_price)}</b></div><div className="effect"><span>Стоимость улучшения</span><b>{money(stadium.upgrade_cost)}</b></div><Button disabled={loading||stadium.upgrade_cost==null} onClick={async()=>{setLoading(true);setError('');try{const r=await upgradeStadium();notify(`Стадион улучшен до ${r.level} уровня`);setStadium(await getStadiumInfo());setClub(await getClub(initialClub.id));}catch(e){setError(errorText(e));}finally{setLoading(false);}}}>{stadium.upgrade_cost==null?'Максимальный уровень':'Улучшить стадион'}</Button></>:<p className="muted">{loading?'Загрузка стадиона…':'Данные стадиона недоступны.'}</p>}</Card></>}
    {page==='match'&&<><Header title={nextMatch?.status==='LIVE'?'Матч · LIVE':'Матчи'}/><Card><div className="friendly-actions"><Button disabled={friendlyBusy} onClick={async()=>{setFriendlyBusy(true);setError('');try{const r=await playFriendlyMatch('friendly');setLastFriendlyResult(r);notify('Товарищеский матч сыгран');await loadPage('match');}catch(e){setError(errorText(e));}finally{setFriendlyBusy(false);}}}>🤝 Товарищеский матч</Button><Button secondary disabled={friendlyBusy} onClick={async()=>{setFriendlyBusy(true);setError('');try{const r=await playFriendlyMatch('test');setLastFriendlyResult(r);notify('Тестовый матч запущен и завершён');await loadPage('match');}catch(e){setError(errorText(e));}finally{setFriendlyBusy(false);}}}>🧪 Запустить тестовый матч</Button></div>{lastFriendlyResult&&<div className="friendly-result"><small>{lastFriendlyResult.competition_name} · матч #{lastFriendlyResult.match_id}</small><div><b>{lastFriendlyResult.home_name}</b><strong>{lastFriendlyResult.home_score} : {lastFriendlyResult.away_score}</strong><b>{lastFriendlyResult.away_name}</b></div><p>Матч завершён. На таблицу лиги и обычные награды не влияет.</p></div>}<MatchCanvas match={nextMatch}/>{nextMatch?<><h3>{nextMatch.home_name} — {nextMatch.away_name}</h3><p className="muted">{nextMatch.competition_name||nextMatch.status} · {dateText(nextMatch.scheduled_at)}</p>{nextMatch.events?.map(event=><div className="event" key={event.id}><b>{event.minute}′</b><span>⚽</span>{event.description}</div>)}</>:<p className="muted">Матч пока не назначен.</p>}<Button onClick={()=>void loadPage('match')}>Обновить информацию</Button></Card></>}

    {error&&<div className="api-error"><b>Ошибка</b><p>{error}</p><Button secondary onClick={reload}>Повторить</Button></div>}
    {loading&&<div className="skeleton">Обновляем данные…</div>}
    <nav className="bottom-nav">{nav.map(n=><button className={page===n.id?'current':''} key={n.id} onClick={()=>go(n.id)}><span>{n.icon}</span><small>{n.label}</small></button>)}</nav>{toast&&<div className="toast">{toast}</div>}</div>;
}

from pathlib import Path


SERVICES = Path(__file__).parents[1] / 'app' / 'services.py'


def test_quick_test_uses_admin_owned_club_and_does_not_create_a_replacement_club():
    source = SERVICES.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    start = source.index('async def start_quick_test_league')
    end = source.index('\n\nasync def auto_join_rookie_league', start)
    block = source[start:end]

    assert 'SELECT id FROM clubs WHERE owner_user_id=:u ORDER BY is_test ASC,id LIMIT 1' in block
    assert "'creator': int(owner)" in block
    assert "{'l':league_id,'c':int(player_club)}" in block
    assert 'create_test_bot_club' not in block
    assert 'start_global_competitions=False' in block


def test_quick_test_handles_null_season_seed_without_int_none_error():
    source = SERVICES.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    assert "scalar_one_or_none() or 0)" in source
    assert "season_seed=int((await s.execute(text(\"SELECT season_seed FROM seasons WHERE id=:s\"), {'s':int(season_id)})).scalar_one_or_none() or 0)" in source


def test_quick_test_falls_back_to_latest_season_and_handles_null_seed():
    source = SERVICES.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    start = source.index('async def _start_league_by_id')
    end = source.index('\n\nasync def start_quick_test_league', start)
    block = source[start:end]
    assert "SELECT id FROM seasons ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1" in block
    assert "raise ValueError('Нельзя запустить быстрый тест: в базе ещё не создан сезон.')" in block
    assert "scalar_one_or_none() or 0)" in block
    assert "'season_id':int(season_id)" in block

from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]
SERVER = (ROOT / "app" / "server.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
SERVICES = (ROOT / "app" / "services.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
PACKS = (ROOT / "app" / "game" / "packs.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
API = (ROOT / "mini_app" / "src" / "api.ts").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
UI = (ROOT / "mini_app" / "src" / "ClubScreen.tsx").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
CSS = (ROOT / "mini_app" / "src" / "style.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_pack_open_returns_card_fields_and_real_rarity():
    assert "'rarity': str(template['rarity'] or 'BASE').upper()" in PACKS
    assert "'portrait_url': template['portrait_url']" in PACKS
    assert "'potential': int(template['potential'] or _template_ovr(template))" in PACKS
    assert "@app.post(\"/api/packs/open\")" in SERVER


def test_pack_ui_has_animated_reveal_and_rarity_classes():
    assert "pack-opening-overlay" in UI
    assert "player-card " in UI
    assert "rarityClass(lastPackResult.rarity)" in UI
    assert "@keyframes cardReveal" in CSS
    assert "rarity-legendary" in CSS and "rarity-epic" in CSS and "rarity-rare" in CSS


def test_lineup_uses_server_formation_coordinates_and_slot_order():
    assert '"formation_slots": [' in SERVER
    assert "ordered = get_best_xi([dict(r) for r in rows], formation)" in SERVER
    assert "style={{left:`${slot.x}%`,top:`${100-slot.y}%`}}" in UI
    assert "if(preserveLineupSelection.current){preserveLineupSelection.current=false;}else{setLineupSlots(r.formation_slots||[])" in UI
    assert "export type FormationSlot" in API


def test_friendly_and_test_match_share_authorized_simulation_endpoint():
    assert '@app.post("/api/matches/friendly")' in SERVER
    assert '@app.post("/api/matches/test")' in SERVER
    assert "create_user_friendly_match(session, int(club[\"id\"]), mode=payload.mode)" in SERVER
    start = SERVICES.index("async def create_user_friendly_match")
    end = SERVICES.index("async def create_admin_test_match", start)
    block = SERVICES[start:end]
    assert "simulate_match(" in block
    assert "league_id,season_id,round,home_club_id,away_club_id" in block
    assert "status='FINISHED'" in block
    assert "league_id=NULL" in block or "VALUES(NULL,NULL,0" in block
    assert "🤝 Товарищеский матч" in UI
    assert "🧪 Запустить тестовый матч" in UI

====================================================================================================
FILE: MINI_APP_PACK_LINEUP_FRIENDLY_FIX.md
====================================================================================================
# Mini App fixes: pack opening, lineup formation, friendly/test matches

Date: 2026-10-21

## Changes

1. **Player packs**
   - The pack API returns the actual card rarity, OVR, potential, age, nationality, card version and portrait URL from the selected player template.
   - Mini App shows a full player card after opening and a staged opening overlay animation.
   - Card frame/glow is driven by the server's `rarity`: BASE, RARE, EPIC, LEGENDARY. No rarity is inferred from pack number or a frontend random choice.

2. **Starting XI / formation**
   - `/api/lineup` returns the actual slot coordinates from `app/game/board.py`.
   - The pitch renders player markers at the selected formation's coordinates instead of grouping players into four hard-coded rows.
   - The save endpoint validates the selected 11, rejects unavailable players, and stores them in the same formation-slot order consumed by the match engine.
   - AI recommendations return their own formation coordinates, so a recommended 4-2-3-1 does not get drawn using stale 4-3-3 slots.

3. **Friendly and test match**
   - Added authenticated `POST /api/matches/friendly` and `POST /api/matches/test` endpoints.
   - Both run the existing match simulation engine with the user's real club against a reusable isolated test opponent.
   - Match rows are tagged as test/friendly and are not linked to a league or season, so they do not change standings or award normal league rewards.
   - Match events, lineups, tactics, final score and xG are persisted. A short debounce returns the just-completed match if the user repeats a request immediately, avoiding duplicate rows.
   - Added Mini App buttons for friendly and test matches and a result panel.

4. **Regression tests**
   - Added static regression checks for pack rarity/card fields, animation classes, formation slot coordinates, and both match endpoints.
   - Updated an existing source-contract test to match the current query that prioritizes the user's non-test club.

## Validation

- 11 targeted tests passed (`test_minapp_pack_lineup_friendly.py`, `test_quick_test_own_club.py`, `test_database_url_normalization.py`, `test_player_name_diversity.py`).
- All Python source files compile successfully.
- TypeScript/JSX syntax check passed (no TS parser/syntax errors).
- A complete frontend typecheck/build could not be verified in this environment because React/Vite dependencies are absent and `npm install` timed out. The production Dockerfile still builds the Mini App with `npm install && npm run build`; a successful Docker build and live Amvera/database run are not claimed.
- No production database was accessed, no existing clubs/players were deleted, and no migration is required for these changes.

## Deployment note

Deploy the two ZIP parts together, extracted into the same project directory. Do not replace only one part. Verify the Docker build and test the pack, lineup save/reload, friendly match and test match in the deployed Mini App before treating the release as production-verified.
